000356: 	private Vector2 GetRelativeLocalLocation(RaycastHit hit)
000357: 	{
000358: 		Terrain component = hit.transform.gameObject.GetComponent<Terrain>();
000359: 		Vector3 size = component.terrainData.size;
000360: 		Vector3 vector = hit.transform.InverseTransformPoint(hit.point);
000361: 		Vector2 zero = Vector2.zero;
000362: 		zero.x = vector.z / size.z;
000363: 		zero.y = vector.x / size.x;
000364: 		float num = 1f / (float)(component.terrainData.heightmapResolution - 1);
000365: 		float num2 = 1f / (float)(component.terrainData.heightmapResolution - 1);
000366: 		zero.x = Mathf.Round(zero.x / num) * num;
000367: 		zero.y = Mathf.Round(zero.y / num2) * num2;
000368: 		return zero;
000369: 	}
000370: }

000008: 	public static float[] CreateHeightMap(Terrain terrain)
000009: 	{
000010: 		TerrainData terrainData = terrain.terrainData;
000011: 		int heightmapResolution = terrainData.heightmapResolution;
000012: 		Vector3 heightmapScale = terrainData.heightmapScale;
000013: 		float[,] heights = terrainData.GetHeights(0, 0, heightmapResolution, heightmapResolution);

000356: 	private Vector2 GetRelativeLocalLocation(RaycastHit hit)
000357: 	{
000358: 		Terrain component = hit.transform.gameObject.GetComponent<Terrain>();
000359: 		Vector3 size = component.terrainData.size;
000360: 		Vector3 vector = hit.transform.InverseTransformPoint(hit.point);
000361: 		Vector2 zero = Vector2.zero;
000362: 		zero.x = vector.z / size.z;
000363: 		zero.y = vector.x / size.x;
000364: 		float num = 1f / (float)(component.terrainData.heightmapResolution - 1);
000365: 		float num2 = 1f / (float)(component.terrainData.heightmapResolution - 1);
000366: 		zero.x = Mathf.Round(zero.x / num) * num;
000367: 		zero.y = Mathf.Round(zero.y / num2) * num2;
000368: 		return zero;
000369: 	}
000370: }
